### 853. TSU-0006370  [Soteriology]  (flags: rhetorical_question, quote_attribution)
- **원문**: But if regeneration precede our coming to Christ, then " men are excusable, it is supposed, in not coming ; and it must be absurd to exhort them to it while they are unregenc- rate.'* (p. 22.) If I understand this reasoning, the amount of it is this : If men are so bad, that none but God can turn their hearts, then their badness becomes excusable ; and if, in our exhorting them, no hope is to be placed in them^ then neither is there any to be placed in God ." Were I to enter the com- pany of a malicious rebel, with a view to persuade him to go and cast himself at the feet of his abused sovereign, I should have no hope of succeeding, or of bringing him to a compli- ance, while he remained under the dominion of such a spirit. < AVhy, then,* it may be asked, ^ do you exhort him to it, till you see his spirit changed ?' Why ?
- **claim**: 인간의 회심은 하나님의 섭리와 인간의 노력 사이에 존재하며, 인간이 먼저 하나님께 나아가기 전에 회심이 먼저 일어나야 한다.

### 1417. TSU-0007755  [Sanctification]  (flags: rhetorical_question, quote_attribution)
- **원문**: " Perhaps," says Mr. T. ** these premises might be fairly disputed." (XIII. 130.) That they might be disputed, is true ; but surely not by Mr. T, He does not profess, that grace is provided sufficient to enable men to keep the law, but barely to comply with the gospel. (XIII. 61.) And surely he cannot dispute our being exhorted to it : what meaning else is there in the above -cited passages?
- **claim**: 그의 전제는 정당하게 논쟁의 여지가 있을 수 있지만, 그는 은혜가 법을 지키기 위해 충분히 제공된다고 주장하지 않으며, 오직 복음에 순종하기 위해 제공된다고 말한다.
